\displaystyle\varepsilon\int_{\Omega}p^{\prime}(\rho^{\rm a})[(\eta\mathfrak{w})_{y}]_{\varepsilon}{\rm div\,}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|p^{\prime}(\rho^{\rm a})\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|(\eta\mathfrak{w}_{y})\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|{\rm div\,}R\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)},

and

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}p(\rho^{\rm a})R\cdot\nabla{\rm div\,}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}-\int_{\Omega}(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))(\nabla u^{\rm a}+\nabla R):\nabla R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\Omega}\Bigl((\nabla p(\rho^{\rm a}))\cdot R\cdot\nabla R+(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))\nabla u^{\rm a}:\nabla R+p(\rho)\nabla R:\nabla R\Bigr)\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla p(\rho^{\rm a})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla u^{\rm a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|(\rho-\rho^{\rm a})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

By substituting the above estimates into (
7.14
) and using Theorem
1.1
, we find

(7.15)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\nabla(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{\Omega}(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a})){\rm div\,}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle+Ce^{-\alpha t}\bigl(\|\varrho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)+C\|\nabla R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We further observe that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\rho R\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\nabla u^{\rm a}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\rho|R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\frac{1}{12}\int_{\Omega}\rho(\mathfrak{D}_{t}R)^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y},

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\varrho\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}\bigr)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq 2\bigl\|\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}\bigr)\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\rho^{-1}\varrho^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\frac{1}{12}\int_{\Omega}\rho(\mathfrak{D}_{t}R)^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y},

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}G\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq 2\int_{\Omega}\rho^{-1}|G|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\frac{1}{12}\int_{\Omega}\rho(\mathfrak{D}_{t}R)^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}.

This together with (
2.10
) and
(
2.21
) that

(7.16)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigl(\rho R\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}+\varrho\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}\bigr)-G\Bigr)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\leq Ce^{-\alpha t}\varepsilon+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Omega}\rho(\mathfrak{D}_{t}R)^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}.

By inserting the estimates (
7.11
), (
7.12
), (
7.15
) and (
7.16
) into (
7.10
)
and
and integrating the resulting inequality over
[0,t]
, we achieve

(7.17)

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\bigl(\mu|\nabla R|^{2}+\mu^{\prime}|{\rm div\,}R|^{2}\bigr)\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\rho|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\,{\rm d}t^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon+\int_{\Omega}(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a})){\rm div\,}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+4C\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla R|^{3}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\,{\rm d}t^{\prime}+C\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\,{\rm d}t^{\prime}.

Notice that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a})){\rm div\,}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{-1}\int_{\Omega}|p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a})|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\frac{\mu}{4}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq C\mu^{-1}\|\varrho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{4}\|\nabla R\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

from which and (
2.21
), we deduce (
7.9
) from (
7.17
).
This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

7.3.
Estimates of
\mathfrak{D}_{t}R

Lemma 7.3
.

For each
t<T^{\star}
, one has

(7.18)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|D_{t}R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\mathfrak{D}_{t}R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\,{\rm d}t^{\prime}\leq C\varepsilon+C\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla R|^{4}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\,{\rm d}t^{\prime}.

Proof.

By applying
\mathfrak{D}_{t}
to the
R
equation of (
2.13
) and taking
L^{2}
inner product of the resulting
equation with
\mathfrak{D}_{t}R,
we find

(7.19)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigl(

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{t}(\rho\mathfrak{D}_{t}R)-\mu\mathfrak{D}_{t}\Delta R-\mu^{\prime}\mathfrak{D}_{t}\nabla{\rm div\,}R+\mathfrak{D}_{t}\nabla\big(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a})\big)

\displaystyle+\mathfrak{D}_{t}\big(\rho R\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}\big)+\mathfrak{D}_{t}\big(\varrho(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\big)\Bigr)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}=\int_{\Omega}\mathfrak{D}_{t}G\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}.

Let us now handle term by term above. Notice that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{t}(\rho D_{t}R)=\rho\mathfrak{D}_{t}^{2}R+(\mathfrak{D}_{t}\rho)\mathfrak{D}_{t}R.

Firstly,

(7.20)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\rho\mathfrak{D}_{t}^{2}R\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{\Omega}\rho|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}.

By virtue of (
1.1
), we write

(7.21)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{t}\rho\mathfrak{D}_{t}R

\displaystyle={\rm div\,}u(-\rho\mathfrak{D}_{t}R)

\displaystyle={\rm div\,}u\big(-\mu\Delta R-\mu^{\prime}\nabla{\rm div\,}R+\nabla\big(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a})\big)+\rho R\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}+\varrho(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})-G\big).

We shall postpone the estimate of the above terms below.

For
i=1,2,
we write

\displaystyle-\mu\mathfrak{D}_{t}\Delta R_{i}

\displaystyle=-\mu\Delta\partial_{t}R_{i}-\mu{\rm div\,}\big((u\cdot\nabla)\nabla R_{i}\big)+\mu\nabla u_{j}\cdot\partial_{j}\nabla R_{i}

\displaystyle=-\mu\Delta\mathfrak{D}_{t}R_{i}+\mu{\rm div\,}\big(\nabla u_{j}\cdot\partial_{j}R_{i}\big)+\mu\nabla u_{j}\cdot\partial_{j}\nabla R_{i}.

The last term above can be written as

\displaystyle\mu\nabla u_{j}\cdot\partial_{j}\nabla R_{i}=\mu\partial_{k}u_{j}\partial_{j}\partial_{k}R_{i}

\displaystyle=\mu\partial_{j}\big(\partial_{k}u_{j}\partial_{k}R_{i}\big)-\mu\partial_{k}\partial_{j}u_{j}\partial_{k}R_{i}

\displaystyle=\mu\partial_{j}\big(\partial_{k}u_{j}\partial_{k}R_{i}\big)-\mu\partial_{k}\big(\partial_{j}u_{j}\partial_{k}R_{i}\big)+\mu{\rm div\,}u\Delta R_{i}.

We observe that the last term above cancels with the first term on the right-side of (
7.21
),
so that there holds

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}\big({\rm div\,}u\Delta R+\mathfrak{D}_{t}\Delta R\big)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\Omega}\Delta D_{t}R\cdot D_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\int_{\Omega}\Big(\partial_{k}\big(\partial_{k}u_{j}\partial_{j}R\big)+\partial_{j}\big(\partial_{k}u_{j}\partial_{k}R\big)-\partial_{k}\big(\partial_{j}u_{j}\partial_{k}R\big)\Big)\cdot D_{t}R_{i}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}.

We get, by using integration by parts, that

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}\Delta\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}=\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\mathfrak{D}_{t}R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y},

and

\displaystyle\mu\int_{\Omega}\Big(\partial_{k}\big(\partial_{k}u_{j}\partial_{j}R\big)+\partial_{j}\big(\partial_{k}u_{j}\partial_{k}R\big)-\partial_{k}\big(\partial_{j}u_{j}\partial_{k}R\big)\Big)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq 12\mu\int_{\Omega}|\nabla u|^{2}|\nabla R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\frac{\mu}{8}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\mathfrak{D}_{t}R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq 12\mu\int_{\Omega}\bigl(\|\nabla u^{\rm a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+|\nabla R|^{2}\bigr)|\nabla R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\frac{\mu}{8}\|\nabla\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Thus, we get, by applying Theorem
1.1
, that

(7.22)

\displaystyle-\mu\int_{\Omega}\big({\rm div\,}u\Delta R

\displaystyle+\mathfrak{D}_{t}\Delta R\big)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{7}{8}\mu\|\nabla\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-Ce^{-\alpha t}\|\nabla R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-12\mu\int_{\Omega}|\nabla R|^{4}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}.

Similar argument leads to

(7.23)

\displaystyle-\mu^{\prime}\int_{\Omega}\big({\rm div\,}u(\nabla{\rm div\,}R)

\displaystyle+\mathfrak{D}_{t}\nabla{\rm div\,}R)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{7}{8}\mu^{\prime}\|{\rm div\,}\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-Ce^{-\alpha t}\|\nabla R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-12\mu^{\prime}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla R|^{4}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}.

To handle the pressure related terms, we write

\displaystyle({\rm div\,}u)\nabla(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))+(u\cdot\nabla)\nabla(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))

\displaystyle=\partial_{j}\big(u_{j}\nabla(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))\big)=\nabla{\rm div\,}\big(u(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))\big)-\partial_{j}\big(\nabla u_{j}(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))\big).

In view of the continuity equations of (
1.1
) and (
2.8
), one has

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))=

\displaystyle-{\rm div\,}\big(u(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))+p(\rho^{\rm a})R\big)

\displaystyle-a(\gamma-1)\big(\rho^{\gamma}{\rm div\,}u-(\rho^{\rm a})^{\gamma}{\rm div\,}u^{\rm a}\big)-\varepsilon p^{\prime}(\rho^{\rm a})[(\eta\mathfrak{w})_{y}]_{\varepsilon}.

As a consequence, we deduce that

\displaystyle({\rm div\,}u)\nabla(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))+\mathfrak{D}_{t}\nabla\big(p(\rho)

\displaystyle-p(\rho^{\rm a})\big)=-\partial_{j}\big(\nabla u_{j}(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))\big)-\nabla{\rm div\,}(p(\rho^{\rm a})R)

\displaystyle-a(\gamma-1)\nabla\big(\rho^{\gamma}{\rm div\,}u-(\rho^{\rm a})^{\gamma}{\rm div\,}u^{\rm a}\big)-\varepsilon\nabla\big(p^{\prime}(\rho^{\rm a})[(\eta\mathfrak{w})_{y}]_{\varepsilon}\big),

from which and Theorem
1.1
, we deduce that

(7.24)

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Big(({\rm div\,}u)\nabla(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))+\mathfrak{D}_{t}\nabla\big(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a})\big)\Big)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega}(p(\rho)-p(\rho^{\rm a}))(\nabla u^{\rm a}+\nabla R):\nabla\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\int_{\Omega}\Bigl((p(\rho^{\rm a}){\rm div\,}R+\nabla p(\rho^{\rm a})\cdot R)

\displaystyle+a(\gamma-1)\big(\rho^{\gamma}{\rm div\,}R+(\rho^{\gamma}-(\rho^{\rm a})^{\gamma}){\rm div\,}u^{\rm a}\big)+\varepsilon p^{\prime}(\rho^{\rm a})[(\eta\mathfrak{w})_{y}]\Bigr){\rm div\,}\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{-\alpha t}\bigl(\varepsilon+\|\varrho|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)+C\|\nabla R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{2}\|\nabla\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

On the other hand, thanks to the continuity equation of (
1.6
), we infer

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\big(\mathfrak{D}_{t}(\rho R\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})+({\rm div\,}u)\rho R\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}\big)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega}\big(\rho(\mathfrak{D}_{t}R)\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}+\rho R\cdot D_{t}\nabla u^{\rm a}\big)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq\bigl(\|\nabla u^{\rm a}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{t}\nabla u^{\rm a}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|u^{\rm a}\nabla^{2}u^{\rm a}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla^{2}u^{\rm a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{\Omega}\rho\left(|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R|^{2}+|R|^{2}\right)\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\|\nabla^{2}u^{\rm a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\Omega}\rho|R|^{4}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}.

It follows from the Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality and (
2.21
) that

\displaystyle\|R\|_{L^{4}(\Omega)}^{4}\leq C\|R\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}\|R\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{2}\leq C\varepsilon^{2}+C\varepsilon\|\nabla R\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}.

As a result, we get, by applying Theorem
1.1
, that

(7.25)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\big(\mathfrak{D}_{t}(\rho R\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})

\displaystyle+({\rm div\,}u)\rho R\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}\big)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{-\alpha t}\Bigl(\varepsilon\bigl(1+\|\nabla R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)+\int_{\Omega}\rho|D_{t}R|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\Bigr).

In view of the
\varrho
equation of (
2.13
), we write

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{t}\big(\varrho\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)+{\rm div\,}u\big(\varrho\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)

\displaystyle=\bigl(\mathfrak{D}_{t}\varrho+\varrho{\rm div\,}u\bigr)\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)+\varrho\mathfrak{D}_{t}\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)

\displaystyle=-\big({\rm div\,}(\rho^{\rm a}R)+\varepsilon[(\eta\mathfrak{w})_{y}]_{\varepsilon}\big)\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)+\varrho\mathfrak{D}_{t}\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr).

It is easy to observe that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\big({\rm div\,}(\rho^{\rm a}R)+\varepsilon[(\eta\mathfrak{w})_{y}]_{\varepsilon}\big)\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq\bigl\|\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl\|\rho^{\rm a}{\rm div\,}R+R\cdot\nabla\rho^{\rm a}+\varepsilon[(\eta\mathfrak{w})_{y}]_{\varepsilon}\bigr\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{-\alpha t}\bigl(\|R\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla R\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigr)\|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\varrho\partial_{t}\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\leq{\bigl\|\partial_{t}\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}}}\|\varrho\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\varrho u\cdot\nabla\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}\big(\bigl\|u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\varrho\|_{L^{2}}+\|\varrho\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl\|\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}}\|R\|_{L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{-\alpha t}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|D_{t}R\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}.

Therefore, we obtain

(7.26)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigl(\mathfrak{D}_{t}\big(\varrho\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)+{\rm div\,}u\big(\varrho\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}

\displaystyle+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)\Bigr)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{-\alpha t}\varepsilon+Ce^{-\alpha t}\|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Omega)}.

We remark that it is exactly the term
{\bigl\|\partial_{t}\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}}}
that needs the highest regularity of the approximate solution. It follows from (
1.12
) that
{\bigl\|\partial_{t}\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\bigr)\bigr\|_{L^{\infty}}}\leq C.

Finally, thanks to (
2.10
), we compute

(7.27)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\big(\partial_{t}G+{\rm div\,}(uG)\big)\cdot\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{t}G\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}+\|G\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|u^{\rm a}\|_{L^{2}}+\|R\|_{L^{2}})\|\nabla\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\alpha t}\|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\alpha t}\|\nabla\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon e^{-\alpha t}+Ce^{-\alpha t}\|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\|\nabla\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

By inserting the estimates (
7.20
) and (
7.22
–
7.27
) into (
7.19
) and then
integrating the resulting inequality over
[0,t],
we arrive at

(7.28)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla\mathfrak{D}_{t}R(t^{\prime})\|^{2}\,{\rm d}t^{\prime}\leq C\varepsilon+C\int_{0}^{t}|\nabla R|^{4}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\alpha t^{\prime}}\|D_{t}R(t^{\prime})\|^{2}\,{\rm d}t^{\prime}.

Applying Gronwall’s inequality leads to (
7.18
).
This finishes the proof of Lemma
7.3
.
∎

7.4.
Estimates for the vorticity and proof of Proposition
2.7

For each vector valued function
v=(v_{1},v_{2})^{\rm T}:\mathbb{R}^{2}\to\mathbb{R}^{2}
, we define its vorticity
{\rm curl\,}v
as follows:

{\rm curl\,}v\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\nabla^{\perp}\cdot v=\partial_{y}v_{1}-\partial_{x}v_{2},\quad\nabla^{\perp}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\begin{pmatrix}\partial_{y}\\
-\partial_{x}\end{pmatrix}.

In particular, we denote
\omega\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}{\rm curl\,}R
.
Applying
{\rm curl\,}
to
\eqref{CNS-error-new}_{2}
gives

(7.29)

\displaystyle{\rm curl\,}(\rho\mathfrak{D}_{t}R)-\mu\Delta\omega+{\rm curl\,}\big(\rho R\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}\big)+{\rm curl\,}\big(\varrho(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a})\big)={\rm curl\,}G.

Lemma 7.4
.

Let
\omega\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}{\rm curl\,}R
. Then there holds

(7.30)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\omega|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\leq C\varepsilon+C\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla R|^{4}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y},

and

(7.31)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\omega|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\,{\rm d}t^{\prime}\leq C\varepsilon+C\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla R|^{3}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\,{\rm d}t^{\prime}.

Proof.

We first get, by taking
L^{2}
inner product of (
7.29
) with
\omega
and using integration by parts, that

\displaystyle\mu\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\omega|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}=\int_{\Omega}\Bigl(

\displaystyle-G\cdot\nabla^{\perp}\omega+\rho\mathfrak{D}_{t}R\cdot\nabla^{\perp}\omega

\displaystyle+\rho R\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla^{\perp}\omega+\varrho\bigl(\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}\bigr)\cdot\nabla^{\perp}\omega\Bigr)\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}.

Applying Young’s inequality gives

\displaystyle\mu\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\omega|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\leq\frac{4}{\mu}\int_{\Omega}\Big(

\displaystyle|G|^{2}+\rho^{2}|\mathfrak{D}_{t}R|^{2}+|\nabla u^{\rm a}|^{2}\rho^{2}|R|^{2}

\displaystyle+\varrho^{2}\bigl|\partial_{t}u^{\rm a}+u^{\rm a}\cdot\nabla u^{\rm a}\bigr|^{2}\Big)\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}+\frac{\mu}{2}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\omega|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y},

which together with Proposition
2.6
, Lemmas
7.2
and
7.3
, and Theorem

1.1
ensures (
7.30
) and (
7.31
).
∎

By summarizing Proposition
2.6
, Lemmas
7.2
,
7.3
and
7.4
, we conclude the proof of
Proposition
2.7
.

Appendix A
Proof of Proposition
2.1

Proof of Proposition
2.1
.

By summarizing Propositions
3.1
,
3.2
,
3.4
,
3.5

and
3.6
, we conclude that (
1.11
) holds and there exist two positive constants
C
and
\alpha
solely depending
on
(a,\gamma,\nu,\bar{\varsigma}_{0},\underline{\varsigma}_{0},\bar{E}_{10})
such that

(A.1)

\displaystyle\|(\eta-1,w)(t)\|_{H^{1}_{\rm h}}+\|(\eta-1,w)(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\rm h}}+\|D_{t}w(t)\|_{L^{2}_{\rm h}}\leq CE_{20}^{\frac{1}{2}}(y)e^{-\alpha t},\quad\forall\,t\in\mathbb{R}^{+},\ y\in\mathbb{R},

from which and (
1.6
), we infer

(A.2)

\displaystyle\|\eta_{t}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{\rm h}}+\|(w_{xx},w_{t})(t)\|_{L^{2}_{\rm h}}\leq CE_{20}^{\frac{1}{2}}(y)e^{-\alpha t},\quad\forall\,t\in\mathbb{R}^{+},\ y\in\mathbb{R}.

By virtue of Sobolev embedding, we obtain

(A.3)

\displaystyle\|\eta(t)\|_{C^{0,\frac{1}{2}}_{\rm h}}+\|w(t)\|_{C^{1,\frac{1}{2}}_{\rm h}}\leq CE_{20}^{\frac{1}{2}}(y)e^{-\alpha t},\quad\forall\,t\in\mathbb{R}^{+},\ y\in\mathbb{R}.

To derive decay estimates for higher order norms, we are going to use induction method. Let
n\in\mathbb{Z}_{+},\ 1\leq n\leq 3,
we denote

(A.4)

\displaystyle

\displaystyle E_{m+2}(t,y)\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\|(\eta-1)(t)\|_{H^{m+1}_{\rm h}}^{2}+\|w(t)\|_{H^{m+2}_{\rm h}}^{2}+\|(\partial_{t}^{m}\eta,\partial_{t}^{m}w)(t)\|_{L^{2}_{\rm h}}^{2},

\displaystyle D_{m+2}(t,y)\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\|(\eta-1)(t)\|_{H^{m+1}_{\rm h}}^{2}+\|w(t)\|_{H^{m+2}_{\rm h}}^{2}+\|D_{t}w(t)\|_{H^{m+1}_{\rm h}}^{2}.

and assume that for
0\leq m\leq n-1,
there exists
\mathcal{F}_{m}(t,y)
so that

(A.5a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{m}(t,y)\sim\bigl(\|(\eta-1,w)(t)\|_{H^{m+1}_{\rm h}}^{2}+\|D_{t}w(t)\|_{H^{m}_{\rm h}}^{2}\bigr),

(A.5b)

\displaystyle\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\mathcal{F}_{m}(t,y)+\delta D_{m+2}(t,y)\leq CE_{m+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t},

(A.5c)

\displaystyle E_{m+2}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t,y)\leq CE_{m+2,0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(y)e^{-\alpha t},

for some
\alpha>0,\ \delta>0
and all
t\in\mathbb{R}^{+},\ y\in\mathbb{R}
, where
E_{m+2,0}(y)\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}E_{m+2}(0,y)
.

As shown in Section
3
, the estimates in (
A.5b
) and (
A.5c
) hold when
m=0
. We would like to prove that (
A.5b
) and (
A.5c
) hold for
m=n
. In order to it, we denote

(A.6)

\eta^{(n)}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\partial_{x}^{n}\eta\quad\hbox{and}\quad w^{(n)}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\partial_{x}^{n}w.

Then it follows from (
1.6
) that
(\eta^{(n)},w^{(n)})
verifies

(A.7)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\eta^{(n)}_{t}+w\eta^{(n)}_{x}+\eta w^{(n)}_{x}=-f_{n},\\
&\eta(w^{(n)}_{t}+ww^{(n)}_{x})-\nu w^{(n)}_{xx}+p^{\prime}(\eta)\eta^{(n)}_{x}=-g_{n},\end{aligned}\right.

where

\displaystyle f_{n}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\sum_{m=1}^{n}w^{(m)}\eta^{(n-m+1)}+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\eta^{(m)}w^{(n-m+1)},

\displaystyle g_{n}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\eta^{(m)}w^{(n-m)}_{t}+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\eta^{(m)}\partial_{x}^{n-m}(ww_{x})+\eta\sum_{m=1}^{n}w^{(m)}w^{(n-m+1)}+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\partial_{x}^{m}(p^{\prime}(\eta))\eta^{(n-m+1)},

from which, the inductive assumption (
A.5c
) and Sobolev embedding theorem, we infer

(A.8)

\displaystyle\|(f_{n},g_{n})(t)\|_{L^{2}_{h}}\leq CE_{n+2,0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(y)e^{-\alpha t}.

In what follows, we shall decompose the proof into the following steps:

Step 1.
Decay estimates for
\|\eta^{(n+1)}\|_{L^{2}_{h}}.

Recalling
\zeta=\eta^{-1}
and (
3.51
), we find

(A.9)

\zeta w^{(n)}_{xx}=D_{t}\zeta^{(n+1)}+\sum_{m=1}^{n}w^{(m)}\zeta^{(n-m+2)}-\sum_{m=1}^{n}\zeta^{(m)}w^{(n-m+2)}\quad\hbox{with}\quad\zeta^{(n)}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\partial_{x}^{n}\zeta.

While
we observe from
\eqref{CNS-1d-n}_{2}
that

\displaystyle D_{t}w^{(n)}-\nu\eta^{-1}w^{(n)}_{xx}+\eta^{-1}p^{\prime}(\eta)\eta^{(n)}_{x}=-\eta^{-1}g_{n}.

By plugging (
A.9
) into the above equality yields

(A.10)

D_{t}(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})-\nu\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{n}w^{(m)}\zeta^{(n-m+2)}-\sum_{m=1}^{n}\zeta^{(m)}w^{(n-m+2)}\Big)+\eta^{-1}p^{\prime}(\eta)\eta^{(n)}_{x}=-\eta^{-1}g_{n}.

By taking
L^{2}_{\rm h}
inner product of
\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})
with (
A.10
), we find

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}p^{\prime}(\eta)\eta^{(n)}_{x}(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle=-\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}w_{x}\eta\bigl(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)}\bigr)^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}w_{x}w^{(n)}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\qquad+\nu\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\Big(\sum_{m=2}^{n}w^{(m)}\zeta^{(n-m+2)}-\sum_{m=1}^{n}\zeta^{(m)}w^{(n-m+2)}\Big)\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\qquad-\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}g_{n}(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})\,{\rm d}{x},

from which, and the induction assumption (
A.5c
), we infer

(A.11)

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}p^{\prime}(\eta)\eta^{(n)}_{x}(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq C\|w_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+2\delta^{-1}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta|w_{x}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle+2\nu^{2}\delta^{-1}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta\Big|\sum_{m=2}^{n}w^{(m)}\zeta^{(n-m+2)}-\sum_{m=1}^{n}\zeta^{(m)}w^{(n-m+2)}\Big|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle+2\delta^{-1}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta^{-1}|g_{n}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\delta\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq CE_{m+2,0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(y)e^{-\alpha t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+C\delta^{-1}E_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}

\displaystyle+\delta\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})^{2}\,{\rm d}{x},

where
\delta
is a small positive constant to be determined later on.

While we observe that

\displaystyle\eta_{x}^{(n)}=\partial_{x}^{n}(-\zeta^{-2}\zeta_{x})=-\zeta^{-2}\zeta^{(n+1)}-\sum_{m=1}^{n}\partial_{x}^{m}(\zeta^{-2})\zeta^{(n-m+1)}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}p^{\prime}(\eta)\eta^{(n)}_{x}(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})\,{\rm d}{x}=\nu^{-1}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}p^{\prime}(\eta)\eta^{2}\bigl(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)}\bigr)^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle-\nu^{-1}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}p^{\prime}(\eta)\eta^{2}w^{(n)}(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})\,{\rm d}{x}-\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}p^{\prime}(\eta)\sum_{m=1}^{n}\partial_{x}^{m}(\zeta^{-2})\zeta^{(n-m+1)}(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})\,{\rm d}{x}.

By inserting the above equality into (
A.11
) and using (
1.11
), we deduce that

(A.12)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\nu^{-1}p^{\prime}(\underline{\eta})\underline{\eta}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CE_{n+2,0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(y)e^{-\alpha t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+C\delta^{-1}E_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}

\displaystyle+\delta\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}.

Choosing
\delta=\min\{1,\nu^{-1}p^{\prime}(\underline{\eta})\underline{\eta}/2\}
in (
A.12
) and applying Gronwall’s inequality gives

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta(w^{(n)}-\nu\zeta^{(n+1)})^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t},

which together with (
A.5c
) ensures that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|\zeta^{(n+1)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}.

This together together with the inductive assumption (
A.5c
) leads to

(A.13)

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|\eta^{(n+1)}|^{2}\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}.

And then we deduce from (
A.7
) that

(A.14)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\eta^{(n)}\|_{L^{2}_{h}}=\|\partial_{x}^{n}(\eta w)\|_{L^{2}_{h}}\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}.

Step 2.
Decay estimates of
\|D_{t}w^{(n)}\|_{L^{2}_{h}}.

We first get,
by taking
L^{2}_{\rm h}
inner product of
\eta^{-1}w^{(n)}_{xx}
with the
w^{(n)}
equation of (
A.7
), that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|w^{(n)}_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\nu\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta^{-1}|w^{(n)}_{xx}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}=\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\bigl(g_{n}-p^{\prime}(\eta)\eta^{(n)}_{x}+\eta ww_{x}^{(n)}\bigr)\eta^{-1}w^{(n)}_{xx}\,{\rm d}{x}.

Thanks to (
1.11
), (
A.5c
) and (
A.14
), we infer

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|w^{(n)}_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\nu\bar{\eta}^{-1}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|w^{(n)}_{xx}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\leq\frac{1}{2}\nu\bar{\eta}^{-1}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|w^{(n)}_{xx}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}.

This leads to

(A.15)

\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|w^{(n)}_{x}|^{2}+\nu\bar{\eta}^{-1}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|w^{(n)}_{xx}|^{2}\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}.

While we get,
by taking
L^{2}_{\rm h}
inner product of
D_{t}w^{(n)}
with the
w^{(n)}
equation of (
A.7
), that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}-\nu\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}w^{(n)}_{xx}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}=-\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta^{-1}g_{n}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}-\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}p^{\prime}(\eta)\eta^{(n)}_{x}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}.

Observing that

\displaystyle-\nu\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}w^{(n)}_{xx}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle=-\nu\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}w^{(n)}_{xx}\partial_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}-\nu\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}w^{(n)}_{xx}ww^{(n)}_{x}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle=\frac{\nu}{2}\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|w^{(n)}_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\frac{\nu}{2}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}(w^{(n)}_{x})^{2}w_{x}\,{\rm d}{x},

which together with (
1.11
), (
A.5c
) and (
A.14
) ensures that

(A.16)

\nu\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|w^{(n)}_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}.

Before proceeding, let us admit the following lemma, the proof of which will be postponed till we finish the proof of
Proposition
2.1
.

Lemma A.1
.

For all
t>0
,
y\in\mathbb{R}
, there holds

(A.17)

\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta|D_{t}w^{(n)}_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\nu\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}+C\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\bigl(|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}+|w^{(n)}_{xx}|^{2}\bigr)\,{\rm d}{x}.

Step 3.
End of the induction and summary of decay estimates

By virtue of (
A.15
), (
A.16
) and (
A.17
),
we can find a large enough constant
A_{7}
such that

\displaystyle\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\bigl(A_{7}|w^{(n)}_{x}|^{2}+\eta|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\bigr)\,{\rm d}{x}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\bigl(|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}+|w^{(n)}_{xx}|^{2}+\nu|(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}|^{2}\bigr)\,{\rm d}{x}\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t},

from which, the induction assumption (
A.5b
) for all
0\leq m\leq n-1
and (
A.12
), we deduce that
(
A.5b
) holds with
m=n
for some small positive number
\delta.
This implies (
A.5c
) with
m=n
. We thus complete the induction argument and show in aprticular that

(A.18)

\displaystyle\|(\eta-1)(t)\|_{H^{4}_{\rm h}}^{2}+\|w(t)\|_{H^{5}_{\rm h}}^{2}+\sum_{m=1}^{2}\|\partial_{t}^{m}\eta(t)\|_{H^{4-m}_{{\rm h}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{m}w(t)\|_{H^{5-2m}_{\rm h}}^{2}\leq CE_{5,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}.

This finishes the proof of Proposition
2.1
.
∎

Now let us present the proof of Lemma
A.1
.

Proof of Lemma
A.1
.

We first rewrite
\eqref{CNS-1d-n}_{2}
as

(A.19)

\begin{split}&\eta D_{t}w^{(n)}-\nu w^{(n)}_{xx}+(p(\eta))^{(n+1)}=-\tilde{g}_{n},\quad\hbox{with}\quad\\
&\tilde{g}_{n}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\eta^{(m)}w^{(n-m)}_{t}+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\eta^{(m)}\partial_{x}^{n-m}(ww_{x})+\eta\sum_{m=1}^{n}w^{(m)}w^{(n-m+1)}.\end{split}

By applying
D_{t}
to (
A.19
) and then taking
L^{2}_{\rm h}
inner product of the resulting equation with
D_{t}w^{(n)},

we find

(A.20)

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\bigl(\eta D^{2}_{t}w^{(n)}+D_{t}\eta D_{t}w^{(n)}-\nu D_{t}w^{(n)}_{xx}+D_{t}(p(\eta))_{n}\bigr)D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}=-\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}D_{t}\tilde{g}_{n}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}.

In what follows, we shall frequently use the estimates (
A.5c
), (
A.13
) and (
A.14
) to handle
the terms above.

We first observe that

(A.21)

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta D_{t}^{2}w^{(n)}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{\rm d}{{\rm d}t}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta|D_{t}w^{(n)}_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}.

For the second term in (
A.20
), direct calculation gives

(A.22)

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}D_{t}\eta D_{t}w^{(n)}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}\leq\|w_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\leq C\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}.

Notice that

D_{t}w^{(n)}_{xx}=(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{xx}-(2w_{x}w^{(n)}_{xx}+w_{xx}w^{(n)}_{x}).

Then, by using integration by parts, we get that

(A.23)

\displaystyle-\nu\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}D_{t}w^{(n)}_{xx}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle=\nu\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\nu(2w_{x}w^{(n)}_{xx}+w_{xx}w^{(n)}_{x})D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\geq\nu\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}|^{2}-C\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\bigl(|w_{xx}|^{2}+|w^{(n)}_{xx}|^{2}+|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\bigr)\,{\rm d}{x}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}D_{t}(p(\eta))^{(n+1)}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}=\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\partial_{t}(p(\eta))^{(n+1)}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}w(p(\eta))^{(n+2)}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle=-\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\partial_{t}(p(\eta))^{(n)}(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}\,{\rm d}{x}-\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}(p(\eta))^{(n+1)}\bigl(w_{x}D_{t}w^{(n)}+w(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}\bigr)\,{\rm d}{x},

from which, we infer

(A.24)

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}D_{t}(p(\eta))^{(n+1)}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|\partial_{t}(p(\eta))^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\frac{\nu}{4}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle+C\bigl(\|w_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}}+\|w\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}}\bigr)\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|(p(\eta))^{(n+1)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\frac{\nu}{4}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}.

It remains to handle the source term in (
A.20
). Indeed in view of (
A.20
),
we find that the most trouble terms are the following ones with highest derivatives on
\eta
or
w
in
\tilde{g}_{n}
:

(A.25)

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\tilde{g}_{n}^{(1)}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\eta^{(1)}w^{(n-1)}_{t}+\eta^{(1)}ww_{x}^{n-1}+\eta w^{(1)}w^{(n)}=\eta^{(1)}D_{t}w^{(n-1)}+\eta w^{(1)}w^{(n)},

\displaystyle\tilde{g}_{n}^{(2)}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\eta^{(n)}+\eta^{(n)}+\eta w^{(n)}w^{(1)}.

Observe that

\displaystyle D_{t}(\eta^{(1)}D_{t}w^{(n-1)})=(D_{t}\eta_{x})D_{t}w^{(n-1)}+\eta_{x}D^{2}_{t}w^{(n-1)}

\displaystyle=D_{t}\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}\big(\nu w^{(n-1)}_{xx}-(p(\eta))^{(n)}-\tilde{g}_{n-1}\big)+\eta_{x}(D_{t}\eta^{-1})\big(\nu w^{(n-1)}_{xx}-(p(\eta))^{(n)}-\tilde{g}_{n-1}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}D_{t}\big(\nu w^{(n-1)}_{xx}-(p(\eta))^{(n)}-\tilde{g}_{n-1}\big).

It follows from (
A.5c
), (
A.13
) and (
A.14
) that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}D_{t}\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}\big(\nu w^{(n-1)}_{xx}-(p(\eta))^{(n)}-\tilde{g}_{n-1}\big)D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq\underline{\eta}^{-1}\|\big(\nu w^{(n-1)}_{xx}-(p(\eta))^{(n)}-\tilde{g}_{n-1}\big)\|_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}\eta_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x},

and

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta_{x}(D_{t}\eta^{-1})\big(\nu w^{(n-1)}_{xx}-(p(\eta))^{(n)}-\tilde{g}_{n-1}\big)D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq\|\eta_{x}(D_{t}\eta^{-1})\big(\nu w^{(n-1)}_{xx}-(p(\eta))^{(n)}-\tilde{g}_{n-1}\big)\|_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}.

Next we deal with the term
\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}D_{t}\big(\nu w^{(n-1)}_{xx}-(p(\eta))^{(n)}-\tilde{g}_{n-1}\big)\,{\rm d}{x}
. We compute

\displaystyle\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}D_{t}w^{(n-1)}_{xx}

\displaystyle=\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}-\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}w_{x}w^{(n)}_{x},

so that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}D_{t}w^{(n-1)}_{xx}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle=\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}-\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}w_{x}w^{(n)}_{x}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}+C\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\frac{\nu}{4}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}D_{t}(p(\eta))^{(n)}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq\underline{\eta}^{-2}\|\eta_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}}^{2}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}(p(\eta))_{n}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}+C\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}.

Finally, by virtue of the definition of
\tilde{g}_{n}
given by (
A.19
),
and the estimates (
A.5c
), (
A.13
) and (
A.14
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\eta_{x}\eta^{-1}D_{t}\tilde{g}_{n-1}D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq\underline{\eta}^{-2}\|\eta_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}}^{2}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}\tilde{g}_{n-1}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}

\displaystyle\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}+C\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}.

By summarizing the above estimates, we arrive at

(A.26)

\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}D_{t}(\eta^{(1)}D_{t}w^{(n-1)})D_{t}w^{(n)}\,{\rm d}{x}\leq CE_{n+2,0}(y)e^{-\alpha t}+\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\bigl(C|D_{t}w^{(n)}|^{2}+\frac{\nu}{4}|(D_{t}w^{(n)})_{x}|^{2}\bigr)\,{\rm d}{x}.

The other terms in (
A.25
) can be handled along the same line.

By substituting the estimates (
A.21
–
A.24
) and (
A.26
) into (
A.20
),
we conclude the proof of (
A.17
). This finishes the proof of Lemma
A.1
.
∎

Appendix B
Proof of Lemma
2.1

Let us recall Lemma
2.1
: let
2<p<\infty
and
\Omega={\mathbb{T}}\times\mathbb{R}
, there exists a constant
C
depending solely on
p
such that for any
f\in H^{1}(\Omega),
there holds

(B.1)

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L^{p}(\Omega)}\leq C\Bigl(\|f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{2}{p}}\|\nabla f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{1-\frac{2}{p}}+\|f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{p}}\|\nabla f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\Bigr).

Proof of Lemma
2.1
.

Let
f\in H^{1}(\Omega),
we split it as follows

f(x,y)=\tilde{f}(x,y)+\bar{f}(y),\quad\hbox{with}\quad\bar{f}(y)\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}f(x,y)\,{\rm d}{x},\quad\hbox{and}\quad\tilde{f}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}f-\bar{f}.

We observe that
\bar{f}\in H^{1}(\mathbb{R})
. Indeed, applying Hölder’s inequality gives

(B.2)

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\|\bar{f}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\bigl|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}f(x,y)\,{\rm d}{x}\bigr|^{2}\,{\rm d}{y}\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|f(x,y)|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}=\|f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{y}\bar{f}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}=\|\overline{\partial_{y}f}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}\leq\|\partial_{y}f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}.

Furthermore,
\bar{f}\in H^{1}(\Omega)
and
\tilde{f}\in H^{1}(\Omega),
and there hold

(B.3)

\displaystyle\|\bar{f}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}=\|\bar{f}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})},\quad\|\nabla_{x,y}\bar{f}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}=\|\partial_{y}\bar{f}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}.

For
\tilde{f}
, we get, by applying the classical two dimensional Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality, that

(B.4)

\displaystyle\|\tilde{f}\|_{L^{p}(\Omega)}\leq C\|\tilde{f}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{2}{p}}\|\tilde{f}\|_{H^{1}(\Omega)}^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\leq C\|\tilde{f}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+C\|\tilde{f}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{2}{p}}\|\nabla\tilde{f}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{1-\frac{2}{p}}.

Notice that

\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}\tilde{f}(x,y)\,{\rm d}{x}=0,\quad\forall y\in\mathbb{R},

by applying Poincaré’s inequality, one has

\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|\tilde{f}(x,y)|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\leq\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|\partial_{x}\tilde{f}(x,y)|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x},\quad\forall y\in\mathbb{R}.

This leads to

(B.5)

\displaystyle\|\tilde{f}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|\tilde{f}(x,y)|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}}|\partial_{x}\tilde{f}(x,y)|^{2}\,{\rm d}{x}\,{\rm d}{y}\leq\|\nabla\tilde{f}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}.

Plugging (
B.5
) into (
B.4
) and using (
B.3
), we find

(B.6)

\displaystyle\|\tilde{f}\|_{L^{p}(\Omega)}\leq C\|\tilde{f}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{2}{p}}\|\nabla\tilde{f}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\leq C\|f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{2}{p}}\|\nabla f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{1-\frac{2}{p}}.

While for
\bar{f}(y),
we get, by applying the one dimensional Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality (see
[
5
,
22
]
) and (
B.2
), that

(B.7)

\displaystyle\|\bar{f}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R})}\leq C\|\bar{f}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{p}}\|\partial_{y}\bar{f}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\leq C\|f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{p}}\|\partial_{y}f\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}.

Then (
B.1
) follows immediately from (
B.6
) and (
B.7
). This finishes proof of Lemma
2.1
.
∎
